# Comparisons

Generated by `tools/results/compare.py` from `data/`. Do not edit this file; change the rows in `data/results/` and run the script again.

7877 rows from 24 boards (TI 19, ST 1, NXP 1, Raspberry Pi 1, Espressif 1, BeagleBoard.org 1). Every number has a source file in its row. Ranges are min to max. "(1 run)" marks a timing not repeated three times.
